While Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a solution by directly optimizing driving goals via reward signals, the rendering-based training environments introduce the rendering gap and are inefficient due to high computational costs. To overcome these challenges, we present a novel Pseudo-simulation-based RL method for closed-loop end-to-end autonomous driving, PerlAD. Based on offline datasets, PerlAD constructs a pseudo-simulation that operates in vector space, enabling efficient, rendering-free trial-and-error training. To bridge the gap between static datasets and dynamic closed-loop environments, PerlAD introduces a prediction world model that generates reactive agent trajectories conditioned on the ego vehicle's plan. Furthermore, to facilitate efficient planning, PerlAD utilizes a hierarchical decoupled planner that combines IL for lateral path generation and RL for longitudinal speed optimization. Comprehensive experimental results demonstrate that PerlAD achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Bench2Drive benchmark, surpassing the previous E2E RL method by 10.29% in Driving Score without requiring expensive online interactions. Additional evaluations on the DOS benchmark further confirm its reliability in handling safety-critical occlusion scenarios.

</details>


### [303] [Learning from Mistakes: Post-Training for Driving VLA with Takeover Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14972)
*Yinfeng Gao,Deqing Liu,Qichao Zhang,Yupeng Zheng,Haochen Tian,Guang Li,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Da-Wei Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TakeVLA的新型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）后训练框架，通过引入接管前语言监督和场景幻想机制，在自动驾驶中提升了安全性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA方法依赖离线训练且易受分布偏移影响，当前的接管数据使用方式存在监督局限和缺乏主动探索的问题。

Method: 引入接管前语言监督以让模型从错误中主动学习，并提出场景幻想这一强化微调范式，在重建的接管场景中鼓励主动探索。

Result: 在Bench2Drive基准上，TakeVLA比SimLingo基线驾驶得分提高4.93，平均TTC提升11.76%，显示出更强的安全性与性能。

Conclusion: TakeVLA有效克服了现有VLA方法在安全边际和探索能力上的不足，为自动驾驶中的后训练提供了更高效、更安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) paradigms in end-to-end autonomous driving rely on offline training from static datasets, leaving them vulnerable to distribution shift. Recent post-training methods use takeover data to mitigate this by augmenting the dataset with high-quality expert takeover samples, yet they suffer from two key limitations: supervision restricted to the period after the takeover moments leads to policies with limited safety margins, and passive preference optimization lacks active exploration for optimal performance. In this paper, we propose TakeVLA, a novel VLA post-training framework that overcomes these shortcomings through two complementary innovations. First, we introduce pre-takeover language supervision, which allows the VLA to learn from mistakes proactively. By explicitly teaching the model about what to do in error-prone situations, we cultivate a precautionary mindset that anticipates hazards early and substantially enlarges safety margins. Second, we propose Scenario Dreaming, a reinforcement fine-tuning paradigm that operates in reconstruceted takeover scenarios, encouraging active exploration beyond mere preference fitting. Experiments on the Bench2Drive benchmark demonstrate that TakeVLA achieves state-of-the-art closed-loop performance, surpassing the strong VLA baseline SimLingo by 4.93 in driving score, with an enhanced safety margin as evidenced by an 11.76% increase in average TTC.

</details>


### [304] [Multi-Mode Pneumatic Artificial Muscles Driven by Hybrid Positive-Negative Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15066)
*Siyuan Feng,Ruoyu Feng,Shuguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可充气流体驱动折纸结构的人工肌肉（IN-FOAMs），具有轻薄、灵活、低成本等优点，可通过正负压调控实现多种运动模式，并展现出在柔性可穿戴设备和软体机器人中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类对仿生机器人运动的追求启发，设计一种能够实现类人灵活运动且适用于可穿戴设备的新型人工肌肉结构。

Method: 采用热压和热封工艺，利用低成本可热封薄膜材料制造包含可编程骨架和外层皮肤的可充气折纸-inspired 结构，并通过正负压联合驱动实现多模态运动；结合实验、理论与数值模拟方法研究其力学行为。

Result: 实现了收缩、弯曲、扭转和旋转等多种运动模式；输出力和收缩程度可通过混合正负压调节；多层骨架结构提升了收缩比；多通道骨架实现了单一肌肉内集成多种运动模式。

Conclusion: IN-FOAMs 具有良好的可调性、灵活性和集成性，适用于未来柔性可穿戴设备和紧凑型软体机器人系统。

Abstract: Artificial muscles embody human aspirations for engineering lifelike robotic movements. This paper introduces an architecture for Inflatable Fluid-Driven Origami-Inspired Artificial Muscles (IN-FOAMs). A typical IN-FOAM consists of an inflatable skeleton enclosed within an outer skin, which can be driven using a combination of positive and negative pressures (e.g., compressed air and vacuum). IN-FOAMs are manufactured using low-cost heat-sealable sheet materials through heat-pressing and heat-sealing processes. Thus, they can be ultra-thin when not actuated, making them flexible, lightweight, and portable. The skeleton patterns are programmable, enabling a variety of motions, including contracting, bending, twisting, and rotating, based on specific skeleton designs. We conducted comprehensive experimental, theoretical, and numerical studies to investigate IN-FOAM's basic mechanical behavior and properties. The results show that IN-FOAM's output force and contraction can be tuned through multiple operation modes with the applied hybrid positive-negative pressure. Additionally, we propose multilayer skeleton structures to enhance the contraction ratio further, and we demonstrate a multi-channel skeleton approach that allows the integration of multiple motion modes into a single IN-FOAM. These findings indicate that IN-FOAMs hold great potential for future applications in flexible wearable devices and compact soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [305] [HALO:Closing Sim-to-Real Gap for Heavy-loaded Humanoid Agile Motion Skills via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15084)
*Xingyi Wang,Chenyun Zhang,Weiji Xie,Chao Yu,Wei Song,Chenjia Bai,Shiqiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于梯度的两阶段系统辨识框架，利用可微分仿真器MuJoCo XLA，在存在未知负载的情况下实现人形机器人从仿真到现实的零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真到现实的强化学习方法在面对未知负载时因模型失配而性能下降，难以在真实场景中有效部署人形机器人。

Method: 构建一个两阶段梯度-based系统辨识框架：第一阶段使用真实数据校准机器人本体模型以减小仿真与现实的内在差异；第二阶段进一步识别未知负载的质量分布；整个过程依托于可微分仿真器MuJoCo XLA进行梯度优化。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中均实现了更精确的参数识别、更高的运动跟踪精度，以及更强的敏捷性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式减少结构化模型偏差，该方法能够在重载条件下实现强化学习策略到硬件的零样本迁移，显著提升实际部署性能。

Abstract: Humanoid robots deployed in real-world scenarios often need to carry unknown payloads, which introduce significant mismatch and degrade the effectiveness of simulation-to-reality reinforcement learning methods. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage gradient-based system identification framework built on the differentiable simulator MuJoCo XLA. The first stage calibrates the nominal robot model using real-world data to reduce intrinsic sim-to-real discrepancies, while the second stage further identifies the mass distribution of the unknown payload. By explicitly reducing structured model bias prior to policy training, our approach enables zero-shot transfer of reinforcement learning policies to hardware under heavy-load conditions. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate more precise parameter identification, improved motion tracking accuracy, and substantially enhanced agility and robustness compared to existing baselines. Project Page: https://mwondering.github.io/halo-humanoid/

</details>


### [306] [BodyGuards: Escorting by Multiple Robots in Unknown Environment under Limited Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15108)
*Zhuoli Tian,Yanze Bao,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BodyGuards的护航框架，用于多机器人系统在对抗性环境中协助操作员安全、高效地完成任务导向型探索，显著降低了操作员风险和任务时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在通信受限的未知且对抗性环境中，保护人类操作员的安全并提高导航效率是当前多机器人协作探索面临的关键挑战。

Method: 提出BodyGuards框架，包含三个核心组件：动态移动策略（维护带风险区域的局部地图）、双模式机器人策略（结合前沿探索与优化返回机制）以及多机器人协同协议（联合规划探索与信息共享）。

Result: 通过大量人机闭环仿真和硬件实验验证，该方法在对抗性和受限环境下显著减少了操作员风险和任务时间，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的任务导向型探索框架能有效平衡安全性、通信限制与探索效率，适用于高风险场景下的多机器人协同护航任务。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are increasingly deployed in high-risk missions such as reconnaissance, disaster response, and subterranean operations. Protecting a human operator while navigating unknown and adversarial environments remains a critical challenge, especially when the communication among the operator and robots is restricted. Unlike existing collaborative exploration methods that aim for complete coverage, this work focuses on task-oriented exploration to minimize the navigation time of the operator to reach its goal while ensuring safety under adversarial threats. A novel escorting framework BodyGuards, is proposed to explicitly integrate seamlessly collaborative exploration, inter-robot-operator communication and escorting. The framework consists of three core components: (I) a dynamic movement strategy for the operator that maintains a local map with risk zones for proactive path planning; (II) a dual-mode robotic strategy combining frontier based exploration with optimized return events to balance exploration, threat detection, and intermittent communication; and (III) multi-robot coordination protocols that jointly plan exploration and information sharing for efficient escorting. Extensive human-in-the-loop simulations and hardware experiments demonstrate that the method significantly reduces operator risk and mission time, outperforming baselines in adversarial and constrained environments.

</details>


### [307] [A Novel Camera-to-Robot Calibration Method for Vision-Based Floor Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15126)
*Jan Andre Rudolph,Dennis Haitz,Markus Ulrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于地面观测移动机器人的新型手眼标定方法，通过设计结合激光跟踪仪和相机的参考板，实现了亚毫米级重复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了将广泛用于精确定位的激光跟踪仪与机器人上的相机结合，实现地面观测测量任务中的高精度标定。

Method: 设计了一种包含反射器巢和相机标定目标的参考板，利用激光跟踪仪获取位姿，并通过机器人搭载的相机观察标定目标，依次估计板位姿、板-相机位姿、机器人位姿，最终计算出机器人-相机变换。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法具有亚毫米级的重复精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的手眼标定方法有效融合了激光跟踪仪和相机的优势，适用于地面观测移动机器人，具备高精度和良好重复性。

Abstract: A novel hand-eye calibration method for ground-observing mobile robots is proposed. While cameras on mobile robots are com- mon, they are rarely used for ground-observing measurement tasks. Laser trackers are increasingly used in robotics for precise localization. A referencing plate is designed to combine the two measurement modalities of laser-tracker 3D metrology and camera- based 2D imaging. It incorporates reflector nests for pose acquisition using a laser tracker and a camera calibration target that is observed by the robot-mounted camera. The procedure comprises estimating the plate pose, the plate-camera pose, and the robot pose, followed by computing the robot-camera transformation. Experiments indicate sub-millimeter repeatability.

</details>


### [308] [Confusion-Aware In-Context-Learning for Vision-Language Models in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15134)
*Yayun He,Zuheng Kang,Botao Zhao,Zhouyin Wu,Junqing Peng,Jianzong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CAICL的混淆感知上下文学习方法，旨在解决基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的机器人操作在混淆对象场景中鲁棒性不足的问题，通过定位和分析混淆源并将其用于提示VLM，显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于VLM存在捷径学习问题，导致其在处理易混淆物体时识别不准，影响机器人操作的可靠性。

Method: 提出CAICL方法，首先进行混淆定位与分析，识别潜在混淆来源，并将该信息作为提示输入VLM，使其关注易致误判的特征。

Result: 在VIMA-Bench上实验表明，CAICL有效缓解了捷径学习问题，成功率达到85.5%，并在不同泛化程度任务中表现出良好稳定性。

Conclusion: CAICL能够显著提升VLM在易混淆场景下的鲁棒性和准确性，为VLM在机器人操作中的应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have significantly improved the generalization capabilities of robotic manipulation. However, VLM-based systems often suffer from a lack of robustness, leading to unpredictable errors, particularly in scenarios involving confusable objects. Our preliminary analysis reveals that these failures are mainly caused by shortcut learning problem inherently in VLMs, limiting their ability to accurately distinguish between confusable features. To this end, we propose Confusion-Aware In-Context Learning (CAICL), a method that enhances VLM performance in confusable scenarios for robotic manipulation. The approach begins with confusion localization and analysis, identifying potential sources of confusion. This information is then used as a prompt for the VLM to focus on features most likely to cause misidentification. Extensive experiments on the VIMA-Bench show that CAICL effectively addresses the shortcut learning issue, achieving a 85.5\% success rate and showing good stability across tasks with different degrees of generalization.

</details>


### [309] [Master Micro Residual Correction with Adaptive Tactile Fusion and Force-Mixed Control for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15152)
*Xingting Li,Yifan Xie,Han Liu,Wei Hou,Guangyu Chen,Shoujie Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为M2-ResiPolicy的主-微残差控制架构，结合高层动作引导与低层实时修正，提升机器人在接触丰富、精细操作任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模仿学习方法在长视野规划上表现良好，但难以感知摩擦变化或即将打滑等关键交互线索，且难以平衡全局任务连贯性与局部反应式反馈。

Method: 采用主-微双策略架构：主策略（MGP）以10Hz频率通过扩散模型生成动作片段，并基于触觉强度动态融合视觉与触觉感知；微校正器（MRC）以60Hz频率利用轻量GRU根据末端力矩反馈进行实时补偿；并结合力混合PBIC执行层调节接触力。

Result: 在易碎物抓取和精密插入等高难度任务中，M2-ResiPolicy显著优于标准扩散策略（DP）和最新反应式扩散策略（RDP），芯片无损抓取成功率达93%，且力控稳定性更优。

Conclusion: M2-ResiPolicy通过多模态感知融合与多时间尺度协同控制，有效提升了接触丰富、精细操作任务中的安全性与成功率。

Abstract: Robotic contact-rich and fine-grained manipulation remains a significant challenge due to complex interaction dynamics and the competing requirements of multi-timescale control. While current visual imitation learning methods excel at long-horizon planning, they often fail to perceive critical interaction cues like friction variations or incipient slip, and struggle to balance global task coherence with local reactive feedback. To address these challenges, we propose M2-ResiPolicy, a novel Master-Micro residual control architecture that synergizes high-level action guidance with low-level correction. The framework consists of a Master-Guidance Policy (MGP) operating at 10 Hz, which generates temporally consistent action chunks via a diffusion-based backbone and employs a tactile-intensity-driven adaptive fusion mechanism to dynamically modulate perceptual weights between vision and touch. Simultaneously, a high-frequency (60 Hz) Micro-Residual Corrector (MRC) utilizes a lightweight GRU to provide real-time action compensation based on TCP wrench feedback. This policy is further integrated with a force-mixed PBIC execution layer, effectively regulating contact forces to ensure interaction safety. Experiments across several demanding tasks including fragile object grasping and precision insertion, demonstrate that M2-ResiPolicy significantly outperforms standard Diffusion Policy (DP) and state-of-the-art Reactive Diffusion Policy (RDP), achieving a 93\% damage-free success rate in chip grasping and superior force regulation stability.

</details>


### [310] [ForceVLA2: Unleashing Hybrid Force-Position Control with Force Awareness for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15169)
*Yang Li,Zhaxizhuoma,Hongru Jiang,Junjie Xia,Hongquan Zhang,Jinda Du,Yunsong Zhou,Jia Zeng,Ce Hao,Jieji Ren,Qiaojun Yu,Cewu Lu,Yu Qiao,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ForceVLA2的端到端视觉-语言-动作框架，通过引入力感知提示和跨尺度专家混合机制，实现机器人在接触丰富操作中的混合力-位置闭环控制，显著提升了任务成功率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于位置控制的具身智能在接触丰富的操作中缺乏对交互力的显式感知与调控，限制了其在真实场景下的稳定性、精度和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出ForceVLA2框架：在视觉语言模型（VLM）专家中引入基于力的提示以构建力感知任务概念，并在动作专家中采用跨尺度混合专家（MoE）结构，自适应融合这些概念与实时交互力信号，实现闭环的混合力-位置控制；同时构建包含1000条轨迹的ForceVLA2-Dataset用于训练与评估。

Result: 实验表明，ForceVLA2在五项接触丰富的任务中相比pi0和pi0.5分别提升48.0%和35.0%的成功率，有效缓解了机械臂过载和接触不稳定等常见失效模式。

Conclusion: ForceVLA2通过显式的力感知与混合控制机制，推动了视觉-语言-动作模型在交互式物理智能方面的发展，为具身智能体在复杂接触任务中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence for contact-rich manipulation has predominantly relied on position control, while explicit awareness and regulation of interaction forces remain under-explored, limiting stability, precision, and robustness in real-world tasks. We propose ForceVLA2, an end-to-end vision-language-action framework that equips robots with hybrid force-position control and explicit force awareness. ForceVLA2 introduces force-based prompts into the VLM expert to construct force-aware task concepts across stages, and employs a Cross-Scale Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) in the action expert to adaptively fuse these concepts with real-time interaction forces for closed-loop hybrid force-position regulation. To support learning and evaluation, we construct ForceVLA2-Dataset, containing 1,000 trajectories over 5 contact-rich tasks, including wiping, pressing, and assembling, with multi-view images, task prompts, proprioceptive state, and force signals. Extensive experiments show that ForceVLA2 substantially improves success rates and reliability in contact-rich manipulation, outperforming pi0 and pi0.5 by 48.0% and 35.0%, respectively, across the 5 tasks, and mitigating common failure modes such as arm overload and unstable contact, thereby actively advancing force-aware interactive physical intelligence in VLAs. The project page is available at https://sites.google.com/view/force-vla2/home.

</details>


### [311] [What Matters for Scalable and Robust Learning in End-to-End Driving Planners?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15185)
*David Holtz,Niklas Hanselmann,Simon Doll,Marius Cordts,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了端到端自动驾驶架构中常见设计模式在闭环性能上的影响，并提出了一个轻量、可扩展的新架构BevAD，在Bench2Drive基准上取得了72.7%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于开环数据集发展的端到端自动驾驶架构在闭环驾驶中表现不佳，需系统性评估常见模块对实际驾驶性能的影响。

Method: 分析高分辨率感知表示、解耦轨迹表示和生成式规划等架构模式的组合影响，并基于发现提出BevAD新架构。

Result: BevAD在Bench2Drive基准上达到72.7%的成功率，展现出良好的数据扩展性。

Conclusion: 常见的感知-规划分离架构存在局限，BevAD通过轻量化设计实现了高性能与可扩展性的平衡，验证了纯模仿学习在闭环驾驶中的潜力。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has gained significant attention for its potential to learn robust behavior in interactive scenarios and scale with data. Popular architectures often build on separate modules for perception and planning connected through latent representations, such as bird's eye view feature grids, to maintain end-to-end differentiability. This paradigm emerged mostly on open-loop datasets, with evaluation focusing not only on driving performance, but also intermediate perception tasks. Unfortunately, architectural advances that excel in open-loop often fail to translate to scalable learning of robust closed-loop driving. In this paper, we systematically re-examine the impact of common architectural patterns on closed-loop performance: (1) high-resolution perceptual representations, (2) disentangled trajectory representations, and (3) generative planning. Crucially, our analysis evaluates the combined impact of these patterns, revealing both unexpected limitations as well as underexplored synergies. Building on these insights, we introduce BevAD, a novel lightweight and highly scalable end-to-end driving architecture. BevAD achieves 72.7% success rate on the Bench2Drive benchmark and demonstrates strong data-scaling behavior using pure imitation learning. Our code and models are publicly available here: https://dmholtz.github.io/bevad/

</details>


### [312] [NavGSim: High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting Simulator for Large-Scale Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15186)
*Jiahang Liu,Yuanxing Duan,Jiazhao Zhang,Minghan Li,Shaoan Wang,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavGSim是一种基于高斯点阵的模拟器，用于生成高保真、大规模的机器人导航环境，支持多GPU开发，并通过视觉-语言-动作模型验证了其在仿真和真实环境中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人学习中对高真实感、大范围导航环境模拟的需求，尤其是在跨房间或整层楼场景下的渲染与物理模拟难题。

Method: 提出NavGSim，基于分层3D高斯点阵框架实现大场景的高质量渲染；引入基于高斯点阵的切片技术提取可导航区域，并提供支持多GPU的API用于场景重建、机器人配置、策略训练与评估。

Result: 使用NavGSim采集的轨迹训练VLA模型后，该模型在仿真和真实环境中均表现出更强的场景理解能力，能有效处理多样化的导航任务。

Conclusion: NavGSim能够高效构建逼真的大规模导航环境，显著提升机器人导航策略的学习效果，具备良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Simulating realistic environments for robots is widely recognized as a critical challenge in robot learning, particularly in terms of rendering and physical simulation. This challenge becomes even more pronounced in navigation tasks, where trajectories often extend across multiple rooms or entire floors. In this work, we present NavGSim, a Gaussian Splatting-based simulator designed to generate high-fidelity, large-scale navigation environments. Built upon a hierarchical 3D Gaussian Splatting framework, NavGSim enables photorealistic rendering in expansive scenes spanning hundreds of square meters. To simulate navigation collisions, we introduce a Gaussian Splatting-based slice technique that directly extracts navigable areas from reconstructed Gaussians. Additionally, for ease of use, we provide comprehensive NavGSim APIs supporting multi-GPU development, including tools for custom scene reconstruction, robot configuration, policy training, and evaluation. To evaluate NavGSim's effectiveness, we train a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model using trajectories collected from NavGSim and assess its performance in both simulated and real-world environments. Our results demonstrate that NavGSim significantly enhances the VLA model's scene understanding, enabling the policy to handle diverse navigation queries effectively.

</details>


### [313] [Coupled Particle Filters for Robust Affordance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15223)
*Patrick Lowin,Vito Mengers,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过耦合两个递归估计器来解耦机器人可操作性估计中视觉、几何和语义歧义的方法，在真实场景数据集上表现出更高的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人在感知输入中因视觉、几何和语义模糊性导致的可操作性估计困难。

Method: 采用两个耦合的递归估计器分别处理可抓取和可移动区域，利用属性特定的规律降低不确定性，并通过双向信息交换聚焦于两者一致的区域。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上，相比Where2Act、Hands-as-Probes和HRP方法，精度分别提升了308%、245%和257%，并在低光或杂乱环境中保持鲁棒性，实际测试成功率达到70%。

Conclusion: 耦合互补估计器能产生精确、鲁棒且符合具身特性的可操作性预测。

Abstract: Robotic affordance estimation is challenging due to visual, geometric, and semantic ambiguities in sensory input. We propose a method that disambiguates these signals using two coupled recursive estimators for sub-aspects of affordances: graspable and movable regions. Each estimator encodes property-specific regularities to reduce uncertainty, while their coupling enables bidirectional information exchange that focuses attention on regions where both agree, i.e., affordances. Evaluated on a real-world dataset, our method outperforms three recent affordance estimators (Where2Act, Hands-as-Probes, and HRP) by 308%, 245%, and 257% in precision, and remains robust under challenging conditions such as low light or cluttered environments. Furthermore, our method achieves a 70% success rate in our real-world evaluation. These results demonstrate that coupling complementary estimators yields precise, robust, and embodiment-appropriate affordance predictions.

</details>


### [314] [A Methodology for Dynamic Parameters Identification of 3-DOF Parallel Robots in Terms of Relevant Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15254)
*Miguel Díaz-Rodríguez,Vicente Mata,Angel Valera,Alvaro Page*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对全并联机器人动态参数辨识的策略，通过从完整动力学模型出发，结合几何简化和对称性考虑，利用加权最小二乘法进行参数识别，并基于统计方法逐步降阶直至满足物理可行性，实验验证了该方法在两种3-DOF并联机器人上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于部分动态参数对机器人动力学贡献较小，在测量噪声和建模误差下难以准确辨识，因此需要一种能有效识别关键参数的方法以提高控制和仿真精度。

Method: 从完整动力学模型出发，结合连杆几何简化和并联机器人支腿对称性进行模型简化，采用加权最小二乘法进行参数辨识，并基于统计分析逐步剔除不显著参数，确保模型物理可实现性。

Result: 该方法在两种实际3-DOF并联机器人上得到实验验证，辨识出的逆动力学和前向动力学响应与实验数据吻合良好。同时提出了一种基于相关参数的前向动力学求解方法用于性能评估。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于相关参数的动态参数辨识策略能够有效提升全并联机器人的建模精度，兼顾模型简洁性与物理可行性，适用于高精度控制与仿真应用。

Abstract: The identification of dynamic parameters in mechanical systems is important for improving model-based control as well as for performing realistic dynamic simulations. Generally, when identification techniques are applied only a subset of so-called base parameters can be identified. More even, some of these parameters cannot be identified properly given that they have a small contribution to the robot dynamics and hence in the presence of noise in measurements and discrepancy in modeling, their quality of being identifiable decreases. For this reason, a strategy for dynamic parameter identification of fully parallel robots in terms of a subset called relevant parameters is put forward. The objective of the proposed methodology is to start from a full dynamic model, then simplification concerning the geometry of each link and, the symmetry due to legs of fully parallel robots, are carried out. After that, the identification is done by Weighted Least Squares. Then, with statistical considerations the model is reduced until the physical feasibility conditions are met. The application of the propose strategy has been experimentally tested on two difierent configurations of actual 3-DOF parallel robots. The response of the inverse and forward dynamics of the identified models agrees with experiments. In order to evaluate the forward dynamics response, an approach for obtaining the forward dynamics in terms of the relevant parameters is also proposed.

</details>


### [315] [HapticVLA: Contact-Rich Manipulation via Vision-Language-Action Model without Inference-Time Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15257)
*Konstantin Gubernatorov,Mikhail Sannikov,Ilya Mikhalchuk,Egor Kuznetsov,Makar Artemov,Ogunwoye Faith Ouwatobi,Marcelino Fernando,Artem Asanov,Ziang Guo,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapticVLA是一种无需依赖实体触觉传感器的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构，通过离线学习触觉感知能力，并利用安全感知的奖励加权流匹配与触觉蒸馏技术，在部署时实现对接触丰富任务的安全、灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA系统在执行需要精细触觉反馈的操作任务时依赖专用触觉硬件，导致成本高且跨平台复现性差。本文旨在通过离线学习方式将触觉感知能力融入VLA模型，从而在推理阶段无需搭载触觉传感器即可完成高精度接触操作。

Method: 提出HapticVLA框架，包含两个阶段：1）安全性感知的奖励加权流匹配（SA-RWFM），训练一个结合预计算触觉奖励的动作专家，以惩罚过大的抓取力和不良轨迹；2）触觉蒸馏（TD），从SA-RWFM教师模型中提取紧凑的触觉token，并训练学生VLA模型仅从视觉和状态信息预测该token，实现在无触觉输入下的触觉感知动作生成。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，HapticVLA实现了86.7%的平均成功率，持续优于包括使用实时触觉反馈在内的基线VLA模型。

Conclusion: HapticVLA成功地将触觉感知能力通过离线蒸馏的方式注入到标准VLA中，消除了部署时对触觉硬件的依赖，同时提升了在接触密集型操作任务中的性能和安全性。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is a crucial capability for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures, as it enables dexterous and safe manipulation in contact-rich tasks. However, reliance on dedicated tactile hardware increases cost and reduces reproducibility across robotic platforms. We argue that tactile-aware manipulation can be learned offline and deployed without direct haptic feedback at inference. To this end, we present HapticVLA, which proceeds in two tightly coupled stages: Safety-Aware Reward-Weighted Flow Matching (SA-RWFM) and Tactile Distillation (TD). SA-RWFM trains a flow-matching action expert that incorporates precomputed, safety-aware tactile rewards penalizing excessive grasping force and suboptimal grasping trajectories. TD further transfers this tactile-aware capability into a conventional VLA: we distill a compact tactile token from the SA-RWFM teacher and train a student VLA to predict that token from vision and state modalities, enabling tactile-aware action generation at inference without requiring on-board tactile sensors. This design preserves contact-rich tactile-aware reasoning within VLA while removing the need for on-board tactile sensors during deployment. On real-world experiments, HapticVLA achieves a mean success rate of 86.7%, consistently outperforming baseline VLAs - including versions provided with direct tactile feedback during inference.

</details>


### [316] [MoE-ACT: Scaling Multi-Task Bimanual Manipulation with Sparse Language-Conditioned Mixture-of-Experts Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15265)
*Kangjun Guo,Haichao Liu,Yanji Sun,Ruhan Zhao,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级多任务模仿学习框架MoE-ACT，用于双臂操作，通过在ACT的Transformer编码器中引入稀疏混合专家（MoE）模块和FiLM调制机制，有效缓解了任务干扰问题，在模拟和真实环境中均比基线模型提升了33%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多任务场景下由于任务间分布差异导致的任务干扰和负迁移问题，提升机器人统一策略在复杂双臂操作中的泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Method: 将稀疏Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模块集成到ACT的Transformer编码器中，利用自适应激活分离多任务动作分布；解码阶段采用FiLM动态调制动作标记，并结合多尺度交叉注意力融合高低层语义特征，同时引入文本指令实现语言条件动作生成。

Result: 在模拟和真实双臂平台上验证，MoE-ACT相比原始ACT平均成功率提升33%，显著增强了多任务性能、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: MoE-ACT通过专家模块分解与动态特征调制，有效解耦多任务表示，为通用双臂操作提供了一个高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The ability of robots to handle multiple tasks under a unified policy is critical for deploying embodied intelligence in real-world household and industrial applications. However, out-of-distribution variation across tasks often causes severe task interference and negative transfer when training general robotic policies. To address this challenge, we propose a lightweight multi-task imitation learning framework for bimanual manipulation, termed Mixture-of-Experts-Enhanced Action Chunking Transformer (MoE-ACT), which integrates sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) modules into the Transformer encoder of ACT. The MoE layer decomposes a unified task policy into independently invoked expert components. Through adaptive activation, it naturally decouples multi-task action distributions in latent space. During decoding, Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) dynamically modulates action tokens to improve consistency between action generation and task instructions. In parallel, multi-scale cross-attention enables the policy to simultaneously focus on both low-level and high-level semantic features, providing rich visual information for robotic manipulation. We further incorporate textual information, transitioning the framework from a purely vision-based model to a vision-centric, language-conditioned action generation system. Experimental validation in both simulation and a real-world dual-arm setup shows that MoE-ACT substantially improves multi-task performance. Specifically, MoE-ACT outperforms vanilla ACT by an average of 33% in success rate. These results indicate that MoE-ACT provides stronger robustness and generalization in complex multi-task bimanual manipulation environments. Our open-source project page can be found at https://j3k7.github.io/MoE-ACT/.

</details>


### [317] [GNIO: Gated Neural Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15281)
*Dapeng Feng,Yizhen Yin,Zhiqiang Chen,Yuhua Qi,Hongbo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Gated Neural Inertial Odometry (GNIO)的新框架，通过引入可学习的运动库和门控预测头来减少低成本惯性传感器在定位中的漂移问题，尤其在静止和复杂运动转换期间表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本MEMS传感器惯性导航因传感器噪声和偏置不稳定性导致快速漂移，现有数据驱动方法在微小漂移和运动模式融合上存在局限。

Method: 提出GNIO框架，包含两个创新：1）可学习的Motion Bank，用于查询全局运动模式字典以获取语义上下文；2）门控预测头，将位移分解为大小和方向，并通过软可微的零速度更新机制抑制噪声。

Result: 在四个公开基准上的实验表明，GNIO显著降低了位置漂移，在OxIOD数据集上轨迹误差减少了60.21%，并在频繁停止和不规则速度等挑战场景中表现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GNIO通过建模运动有效性和上下文信息，有效缓解了惯性导航中的漂移问题，优于现有的CNN和Transformer基线方法。

Abstract: Inertial navigation using low-cost MEMS sensors is plagued by rapid drift due to sensor noise and bias instability. While recent data-driven approaches have made significant strides, they often struggle with micro-drifts during stationarity and mode fusion during complex motion transitions due to their reliance on fixed-window regression. In this work, we introduce Gated Neural Inertial Odometry (GNIO), a novel learning-based framework that explicitly models motion validity and context. We propose two key architectural innovations: \ding{182} a learnable Motion Bank that queries a global dictionary of motion patterns to provide semantic context beyond the local receptive field, and \ding{183} a Gated Prediction Head that decomposes displacement into magnitude and direction. This gating mechanism acts as a soft, differentiable Zero-Velocity Update (ZUPT), dynamically suppressing sensor noise during stationary periods while scaling predictions during dynamic motion. Extensive experiments across four public benchmarks demonstrate that GNIO significantly reduces position drift compared to state-of-the-art CNN and Transformer-based baselines. Notably, GNIO achieves a $60.21\%$ reduction in trajectory error on the OxIOD dataset and exhibits superior generalization in challenging scenarios involving frequent stops and irregular motion speeds.

</details>


### [318] [User-Tailored Learning to Forecast Walking Modes for Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15329)
*Gabriele Abbate,Enrica Tricomi,Nathalie Gierden,Alessandro Giusti,Lorenzo Masia,Antonio Paolillo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的感知模块，利用两个惯性传感器的数据来估计穿戴外骨骼服用户的三种行走模式（平地行走、上下楼梯），并支持在线模型自适应和闭环控制验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使外骨骼服能够适应不同用户，需要具备先进的感知能力，但受限于设备轻便性，传感器配置有限，因此需开发仅依赖少量传感器的高效感知方法。

Method: 采用基于机器学习的方法，仅使用两个惯性传感器的數據，对过去和未来的步行模式进行估计，并实现数据的自我标注以支持在线模型更新。

Result: 在真实生活数据集上的实验表明该方法能有效识别三种行走模式，并成功应用于单用户在线闭环控制实验中。

Conclusion: 所提出的感知模块能够在资源受限的情况下实现准确的步态模式识别，并支持个性化自适应，具有在外骨骼系统中实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: Assistive robotic devices, like soft lower-limb exoskeletons or exosuits, are widely spreading with the promise of helping people in everyday life. To make such systems adaptive to the variety of users wearing them, it is desirable to endow exosuits with advanced perception systems. However, exosuits have little sensory equipment because they need to be light and easy to wear. This paper presents a perception module based on machine learning that aims at estimating 3 walking modes (i.e., ascending or descending stairs and walking on level ground) of users wearing an exosuit. We tackle this perception problem using only inertial data from two sensors. Our approach provides an estimate for both future and past timesteps that supports control and enables a self-labeling procedure for online model adaptation. Indeed, we show that our estimate can label data acquired online and refine the model for new users. A thorough analysis carried out on real-life datasets shows the effectiveness of our user-tailored perception module. Finally, we integrate our system with the exosuit in a closed-loop controller, validating its performance in an online single-subject experiment.

</details>


### [319] [NavThinker: Action-Conditioned World Models for Coupled Prediction and Planning in Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15359)
*Tianshuai Hu,Zeying Gong,Lingdong Kong,XiaoDong Mei,Yiyi Ding,Qi Zeng,Ao Liang,Rong Li,Yangyi Zhong,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavThinker是一种未来感知的社交导航框架，通过结合动作条件世界模型和强化学习，在动态人类环境中实现安全、高效的机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态人类环境中，传统反应式导航方法无法充分考虑行人运动与机器人行为之间的相互影响，导致安全性与效率不足。因此需要一种能够预测未来场景演变并进行联合规划的方法。

Method: 提出NavThinker框架，利用Depth Anything V2的图像块特征空间构建动作条件的世界模型，自回归地预测未来的深度图与行人轨迹；使用多头解码器输出未来状态，并将预测特征融合到当前观测中；采用DD-PPO算法训练策略，并通过预测的人类轨迹进行社交奖励塑形。

Result: 在Social-HM3D数据集上达到最先进的导航成功率，实现了向Social-MP3D的零样本迁移，并成功部署于真实环境中的Unitree Go2机器人，验证了其泛化能力与实际应用性。

Conclusion: NavThinker通过耦合预测与规划，显著提升了机器人在复杂人类环境中的社交导航性能，具备良好的通用性和现实部署潜力。

Abstract: Social navigation requires robots to act safely in dynamic human environments. Effective behavior demands thinking ahead: reasoning about how the scene and pedestrians evolve under different robot actions rather than reacting to current observations alone. This creates a coupled prediction-planning challenge, where robot actions and human motion mutually influence each other. To address this challenge, we propose NavThinker, a future-aware framework that couples an action-conditioned world model with on-policy reinforcement learning. The world model operates in the Depth Anything V2 patch feature space and performs autoregressive prediction of future scene geometry and human motion; multi-head decoders then produce future depth maps and human trajectories, yielding a future-aware state aligned with traversability and interaction risk. Crucially, we train the policy with DD-PPO while injecting world-model think-ahead signals via: (i) action-conditioned future features fused into the current observation embedding and (ii) social reward shaping from predicted human trajectories. Experiments on single- and multi-robot Social-HM3D show state-of-the-art navigation success, with zero-shot transfer to Social-MP3D and real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2, validating generalization and practical applicability. Webpage: https://github.com/hutslib/NavThinker.

</details>


### [320] [MA-VLCM: A Vision Language Critic Model for Value Estimation of Policies in Multi-Agent Team Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15418)
*Shahil Shaik,Aditya Parameshwaran,Anshul Nayak,Jonathon M. Smereka,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MA-VLCM框架，利用预训练的视觉-语言模型作为多智能体强化学习中的中心化批评家，提升样本效率和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体强化学习中中心化批评家学习效率低、泛化差，以及大视觉-语言-动作模型直接部署计算开销大的问题。

Method: 将预训练的视觉-语言模型微调为多智能体行为评估器，作为中心化批评家，输入包括自然语言任务描述、视觉轨迹和结构化多智能体状态信息。

Result: 在分布内和分布外的多智能体团队场景中，不同VLM骨干的模型均表现出良好的零样本回报估计能力。

Conclusion: MA-VLCM显著提升了样本效率，生成适合资源受限机器人部署的紧凑执行策略，同时具备强大多模态推理与零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) commonly relies on a centralized critic to estimate the value function. However, learning such a critic from scratch is highly sample-inefficient and often lacks generalization across environments. At the same time, large vision-language-action models (VLAs) trained on internet-scale data exhibit strong multimodal reasoning and zero-shot generalization capabilities, yet directly deploying them for robotic execution remains computationally prohibitive, particularly in heterogeneous multi-robot systems with diverse embodiments and resource constraints. To address these challenges, we propose Multi-Agent Vision-Language-Critic Models (MA-VLCM), a framework that replaces the learned centralized critic in MARL with a pretrained vision-language model fine-tuned to evaluate multi-agent behavior. MA-VLCM acts as a centralized critic conditioned on natural language task descriptions, visual trajectory observations, and structured multi-agent state information. By eliminating critic learning during policy optimization, our approach significantly improves sample efficiency while producing compact execution policies suitable for deployment on resource-constrained robots. Results show good zero-shot return estimation on models with differing VLM backbones on in-distribution and out-of-distribution scenarios in multi-agent team settings

</details>


### [321] [Zero-Shot Generalization from Motion Demonstrations to New Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15445)
*Kilian Freitag,Alvin Combrink,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯图的新方法，用于整合孤立的专家演示，实现机器人对未见任务的泛化控制，结合了连续动力系统与离散图搜索的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力系统学习方法难以复用孤立演示来生成新行为，限制了在新任务中的泛化能力。

Method: 引入高斯图，将运动原语的空间成分建模为离散顶点并建立连接，提出Stitching和Chaining两种框架以构建具有收敛保证的时间不变或序列式动力系统。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，所提方法能在基线方法失败的情况下成功泛化到新任务。

Conclusion: 高斯图框架有效实现了从少量孤立演示中组合出新颖且稳定的运动策略，提升了机器人任务迁移与组合学习的能力。

Abstract: Learning motion policies from expert demonstrations is an essential paradigm in modern robotics. While end-to-end models aim for broad generalization, they require large datasets and computationally heavy inference. Conversely, learning dynamical systems (DS) provides fast, reactive, and provably stable control from very few demonstrations. However, existing DS learning methods typically model isolated tasks and struggle to reuse demonstrations for novel behaviors. In this work, we formalize the problem of combining isolated demonstrations within a shared workspace to enable generalization to unseen tasks. The Gaussian Graph is introduced, which reinterprets spatial components of learned motion primitives as discrete vertices with connections to one another. This formulation allows us to bridge continuous control with discrete graph search. We propose two frameworks leveraging this graph: Stitching, for constructing time-invariant DSs, and Chaining, giving a sequence-based DS for complex motions while retaining convergence guarantees. Simulations and real-robot experiments show that these methods successfully generalize to new tasks where baseline methods fail.

</details>


### [322] [RoCo Challenge at AAAI 2026: Benchmarking Robotic Collaborative Manipulation for Assembly Towards Industrial Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15469)
*Haichao Liu,Yuheng Zhou,Zhenyu Wu,Ziheng Ji,Ziyu Shan,Qianzhun Wang,Ruixuan Liu,Zhiyuan Yang,Yejun Gu,Shalman Khan,Shijun Yan,Jun Liu,Haiyue Zhu,Changliu Liu,Jianfei Yang,Jingbing Zhang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了面向工业机器人装配操作的Robotic Collaborative Assembly Assistance (RoCo)挑战赛，旨在推动具身人工智能在高精度行星齿轮箱装配任务中的发展。比赛分为仿真和真实世界两阶段，吸引了来自10多个国家的60多支队伍参与，展示了双模型框架和故障恢复课程数据在长视野多任务学习中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动具身人工智能在工业场景中的应用，特别是在人机协作制造环境中实现高精度、连续性的机器人装配操作，减轻人类从事重复性和危险性工作的负担。

Method: 基于Isaac Sim构建自研的数据采集、训练与评估系统，采用双臂机器人进行真实部署；比赛设置仿真轮和真实世界轮，定义细粒度任务阶段以支持分步评分，并使用高质量遥操作数据集进行评估。

Result: 成功举办了RoCo挑战赛，吸引超过60支团队、170名参与者；最优方案如ARC-VLA和RoboCola表现出色；验证了双模型框架在长视野多任务学习中的有效性，并发现利用故障恢复课程数据对实际部署至关重要。

Conclusion: RoCo挑战赛为工业具身智能提供了有效的基准测试平台，证明了先进学习框架与高质量数据在复杂装配任务中的潜力，为未来工业自动化发展指明了方向。

Abstract: Embodied Artificial Intelligence (EAI) is rapidly developing, gradually subverting previous autonomous systems' paradigms from isolated perception to integrated, continuous action. This transition is highly significant for industrial robotic manipulation, promising to free human workers from repetitive, dangerous daily labor. To benchmark and advance this capability, we introduce the Robotic Collaborative Assembly Assistance (RoCo) Challenge with a dataset towards simulation and real-world assembly manipulation. Set against the backdrop of human-centered manufacturing, this challenge focuses on a high-precision planetary gearbox assembly task, a demanding yet highly representative operation in modern industry. Built upon a self-developed data collection, training, and evaluation system in Isaac Sim, and utilizing a dual-arm robot for real-world deployment, the challenge operates in two phases. The Simulation Round defines fine-grained task phases for step-wise scoring to handle the long-horizon nature of the assembly. The Real-World Round mirrors this evaluation with physical gearbox components and high-quality teleoperated datasets. The core tasks require assembling an epicyclic gearbox from scratch, including mounting three planet gears, a sun gear, and a ring gear. Attracting over 60 teams and 170+ participants from more than 10 countries, the challenge yielded highly effective solutions, most notably ARC-VLA and RoboCola. Results demonstrate that a dual-model framework for long-horizon multi-task learning is highly effective, and the strategic utilization of recovery-from-failure curriculum data is a critical insight for successful deployment. This report outlines the competition setup, evaluation approach, key findings, and future directions for industrial EAI. Our dataset, CAD files, code, and evaluation results can be found at: https://rocochallenge.github.io/RoCo2026/.

</details>


### [323] [On the Derivation of Tightly-Coupled LiDAR-Inertial Odometry with VoxelMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15471)
*Zhihao Zhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提供了一种紧耦合LiDAR-惯性里程计的简洁数学公式，基于迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波框架和VoxelMap表示，旨在统一几何建模与概率状态估计，作为技术参考和系统理解的入门指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提供一个清晰且自包含的推导，统一LiDAR-惯性里程计中的几何建模与概率状态估计，并采用一致的符号和显式公式便于理解和应用。

Method: 采用迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波框架，结合VoxelMap表示法，对紧耦合LiDAR-惯性里程计进行数学建模。

Result: 得到了一个结构清晰、符号一致的数学公式体系，有效整合了几何建模与状态估计过程。

Conclusion: 该公式体系可作为紧耦合LiDAR-惯性里程计的技术参考，并有助于初学者理解系统架构与估计原理。

Abstract: This note presents a concise mathematical formulation of tightly-coupled LiDAR-Inertial Odometry within an iterated error-state Kalman filter framework using a VoxelMap representation. Rather than proposing a new algorithm, it provides a clear and self-contained derivation that unifies the geometric modeling and probabilistic state estimation through consistent notation and explicit formulations. The document is intended to serve both as a technical reference and as an accessible entry point for a foundational understanding of the system architecture and estimation principles.

</details>


### [324] [Optimal control of differentially flat underactuated planar robots in the perspective of oscillation mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15528)
*Stefano Lovato,Michele Tonan,Matteo Bottin,Matteo Massaro,Alberto Doria,Giulio Rosati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何结合最优控制与微分平坦控制来改善欠驱动机器人的轨迹跟踪性能，通过优化控制输入和势能减少末端执行器的残余振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动机器人在设计上具有较大的自由度但驱动器较少，利用特定质量分布可实现轻量化和高灵活性，但被动关节的控制需要精确的动力学建模，尤其是在忽略摩擦力时会导致末端执行器在目标位置附近产生残余振荡。

Method: 采用最优控制与微分平坦控制相结合的方法，通过形式化分析和数值仿真验证其有效性，并考虑不同的二次性能指标（如控制 effort 和欠驱动关节的势能）进行优化。

Result: 结果表明，最优控制可用于规划平坦变量，最小化势能可提高对刚度和阻尼变化的鲁棒性，从而减少因刚度/阻尼不匹配引起的振荡。

Conclusion: 结合最优控制与微分平坦控制能够有效提升欠驱动机器人轨迹跟踪的精度和鲁棒性，特别是在存在模型简化（如忽略摩擦）的情况下，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Underactuated robots are characterized by a larger number of degrees of freedom than actuators and if they are designed with a specific mass distribution, they can be controlled by means of differential flatness theory. This structural property enables the development of lightweight and cost-effective robotic systems with enhanced dexterity. However, a key challenge lies in managing the passive joints, whose control demands precise and comprehensive dynamic modeling of the system. To simplify dynamic models, particularly for low-speed trajectories, friction is often neglected. While this assumption simplifies analysis and control design, it introduces residual oscillations of the end-effector about the target position. In this paper, the possibility of using optimal control along with differential flatness control is investigated to improve the tracking of the planned trajectories. First, the study was carried out through formal analysis, and then, it was validated by means of numerical simulations. Results highlight that optimal control can be used to plan the flat variables considering different (quadratic) performance indices: control effort, i.e. motor torque, and potential energy of the considered underactuated joint. Moreover, the minimization of potential energy can be used to design motion laws that are robust against variation of the stiffness and damping of the underactuated joint, thus reducing oscillations in the case of stiffness/damping mismatch.

</details>
